window.addEventListener('load', function(){ const map = map_c5aad6b041c102c339f15f29c5649736; const layers = window.__plantLayers || {}; // <--- key fix (real layer objects) const techOverlays = {}; const railPlantOverlays = {}; const comboOverlays = {}; function isTechLabel(lbl) { return lbl.startsWith("Plants: "); } function isRailLabel(lbl) { return lbl.startsWith("Plants on "); } function isComboLabel(lbl) { return !isTechLabel(lbl) && !isRailLabel(lbl); } // Build overlay dictionaries from the registry Object.keys(layers).forEach((lbl) => { const layer = layers[lbl]; if(!layer) return; if (isTechLabel(lbl)) techOverlays[lbl] = layer; else if (isRailLabel(lbl)) railPlantOverlays[lbl] = layer; else comboOverlays[lbl] = layer; }); // Plant controls (TOP LEFT), staggered L.control.layers(null, techOverlays, {collapsed:false, position:"topleft"}).addTo(map); L.control.layers(null, railPlantOverlays, {collapsed:true, position:"topleft"}).addTo(map); L.control.layers(null, comboOverlays, {collapsed:true, position:"topleft"}).addTo(map); // Offset controls with CSS so you see "three boxes" const style = document.createElement("style"); style.innerHTML = ` .leaflet-top.leaflet-left .leaflet-control-layers:nth-of-type(1) { margin-top: 10px; } .leaflet-top.leaflet-left .leaflet-control-layers:nth-of-type(2) { margin-top: 10px; transform: translateY(140px); } .leaflet-top.leaflet-left .leaflet-control-layers:nth-of-type(3) { margin-top: 10px; transform: translateY(280px); } `; document.head.appendChild(style); // Stats box (BOTTOM LEFT) const box = L.control({position:"bottomleft"}); box.onAdd = function() { const div = L.DomUtil.create("div", "plant-stats-box"); div.innerHTML = `

const m2 = /data-cap='([^']+)'/.exec(html); if(!m1 || !m2) return; const pid = m1[1]; const c = parseFloat(m2[1] || "0"); if(!seen.has(pid)){ seen.add(pid); cap += c; } } catch(e) {} }); }); return {count: seen.size, cap: cap}; } function refresh(){ const r = scanVisiblePlantLayers(); const c = document.getElementById("ps_count"); const p = document.getElementById("ps_cap"); if(c) c.textContent = "Count: " + r.count.toLocaleString();
